Slurm (SSH "Connection closed" before sbatch; no job, empty run directory).
With the gate split into its own torchrun, the MegaMoE serving gate with
DG_SM90_DECODE_PREPARE=1 passed and the selection's process group came up, but
the selection then failed on its first timed step: bench-firmus-megamoe.py's
routing() returns (ids, weights) and the script passed the whole tuple as the
top-k ids, so mega_moe_pre_dispatch_sm90 raised "Mismatched type on argument
#1 ... Expected DLTensor* but got ffi.Array" on all ranks. prologue=1, so the
run again served the control kernels: 9204.30 and 9211.35 are two control
repeats on the new image (vs 9235.28 on image 40197369083).

Fix: unpack the tuple and use routing()'s normalized weights instead of the
separately drawn ones, exactly as bench-firmus-megamoe.py calls the same two
DeepGEMM functions. DG_SM90_DECODE_PREPARE is a JIT template argument read on
every call, so toggling it inside one process selects the two kernels as
intended.
